export const CategoryExplorer = ({tree}) => {
  const {roots, nodes} = tree;
  const [query, setQuery] = useState("");
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState(new Set());
  const [copied, setCopied] = useState(null);
  const matching = useMemo(() => {
    const q = query.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!q) return null;
    const hit = new Set();
    for (const [slug, n] of Object.entries(nodes)) {
      if (slug.includes(q) || n.title.toLowerCase().includes(q)) {
        hit.add(slug);
        let p = n.parent;
        while (p) {
          hit.add(p);
          p = nodes[p]?.parent ?? null;
        }
      }
    }
    return hit;
  }, [query]);
  const isOpen = slug => matching ? matching.has(slug) : expanded.has(slug);
  const toggle = slug => {
    if (matching) return;
    setExpanded(s => {
      const n = new Set(s);
      if (n.has(slug)) n.delete(slug); else n.add(slug);
      return n;
    });
  };
  const copy = slug => {
    if (typeof navigator !== "undefined" && navigator.clipboard) {
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(slug).catch(() => {});
    }
    setCopied(slug);
    setTimeout(() => setCopied(null), 1500);
  };
  const renderNode = (slug, depth) => {
    const n = nodes[slug];
    if (!n) return null;
    if (matching && !matching.has(slug)) return null;
    const hasKids = n.children.length > 0;
    const open = isOpen(slug);
    return <div key={slug}>
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2 py-1 rounded hover:bg-zinc-950/5 dark:hover:bg-white/5" style={{
      paddingLeft: 8 + depth * 16,
      paddingRight: 8
    }}>
          {hasKids ? <button type="button" onClick={() => toggle(slug)} disabled={!!matching} aria-label={open ? "Collapse" : "Expand"} className="w-5 h-5 flex items-center justify-center text-zinc-500 dark:text-white/50 hover:text-zinc-900 dark:hover:text-white disabled:opacity-50 disabled:cursor-default">
              <span className="text-xs">{open ? "▾" : "▸"}</span>
            </button> : <span className="w-5" />}
          <span className="text-sm text-zinc-900 dark:text-white/90 truncate">
            {n.title}
          </span>
          <button type="button" onClick={() => copy(slug)} title={copied === slug ? "Copied!" : "Click to copy slug"} aria-label={`Copy slug ${slug}`} className="text-[11px] px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded bg-zinc-950/5 dark:bg-white/10 text-zinc-700 dark:text-white/80 font-mono whitespace-nowrap cursor-pointer hover:bg-zinc-950/10 dark:hover:bg-white/20 active:bg-zinc-950/15 dark:active:bg-white/25 transition-colors">
            {copied === slug ? "copied!" : slug}
          </button>
        </div>
        {hasKids && open && <div>{n.children.map(c => renderNode(c, depth + 1))}</div>}
      </div>;
  };
  const total = Object.keys(nodes).length;
  return <div className="not-prose my-6">
      <div className="relative">
        <input type="search" value={query} onChange={e => setQuery(e.target.value)} placeholder="Search categories by name or slug..." className="w-full px-4 py-2.5 rounded-lg border border-zinc-950/10 dark:border-white/10 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 text-sm text-zinc-900 dark:text-white placeholder:text-zinc-400 dark:placeholder:text-white/40 focus:outline-none focus:ring-2 focus:ring-[#5D3FD3]/40" />
      </div>
      <div className="text-xs text-zinc-500 dark:text-white/50 mt-2 px-1">
        {total.toLocaleString()} categories
        {matching && <>
            {" · "}
            <span className="text-zinc-700 dark:text-white/70">
              {matching.size.toLocaleString()} matching
            </span>
          </>}
      </div>
      <div className="mt-3 max-h-[600px] overflow-auto rounded-lg border border-zinc-950/10 dark:border-white/10 bg-white dark:bg-zinc-900 py-2">
        {roots.map(slug => renderNode(slug, 0))}
      </div>
    </div>;
};

Every Channel3 category has a stable URL-friendly **slug** (for example `sofas`, `running-shoes`). Pass a slug to filter or look up categories anywhere a category identifier is accepted.

Search the full tree below, or hit `GET /v1/categories/search` to do the same from your code.

<CategoryExplorer tree={categoryTree} />
